3a1(1): @#/7/nas khyod rgyal por bcug pa de'i tshe/bdag la ci zhig bgyi/gzhon nu nor bzangs kyis   
3a2(1): smras pa|ji ltar khyod kyi grogs bram ze nga'i yab kyi mdun na 'don du bcug pa de bzhin du khyod kyang nga'i 
3a3(1): mdun na 'don du gzhug go zhes byas pa'i gtam de gcig nas gcig tu rgyud de/bram ze mdun na 'don 
3a4(1): des thos nas/de 'di snyam du sems te/ngas ci nas gzhon nu nyid kyis kyang rgyal srid mi thob pa de ltar 
3b1(1): bya na|mdun na 'don du zhags pa lta smos kyang ci dgos snyam mo//ci tsam dus gzhan zhig na rgyal po de'i yul na ri  
3b2(1): 'or ba zhig sngo bar gyur nas/rgyal pos de 'bebs su dmag kha cen cig btang nas/de bcom rnam par bcom ste/pham 
3b3(1): par byas nas slar log go/de bzhin du kha cen bdun gyi bar btang ba las/de rnams kyang bcom rnam par bcom ste 
3b4(1): pham par byas nas la slar lhags so//rgyal po chen po rnams la blon po rnams kyis gsol pa/lha ci'i slad du rang dpung  
3b5(1): nyams shing gzhan gyi mthu bskyed par mdzad/gang yang lha'i yul na mtshon gyis 'tsho ba ji snyed mchis pa thams cad dgug du gsol;
4a1(1): @#/ O /bram ze mdun na 'don gyis bsams pa/gzhonanu gsad pa'i thabs bya ba'i dus 'di yin
4a2(1): no snyam nas/des rgyal po la gsol pa/lha de lta bus ni dbab par rngo ma thogs /rgyal pos smras pa/ji ltar byang 
4a3(1): nyid kho na 'gro 'am/mdun na 'don gyis smras pa/ci'i slad du lha nyid gshegs/gzhon na nor bzangs 'di ni/
4a4(1): dar la bab cing mthu rtsal bsnyems pa dang ldan pa legs pas/dmag sa 'di rnams dang lhan cig dgyer gsol/rgyal
4b1(1): pos smras pa/de bzhin du bya'o//zhes de nas rgyal pos gzhon nu bkug nas bsgo ba/gzhon nu dmag dang lhan cig tu ded la ri 'or 
4b2(1): ba rnams pho ba shig/lha bka' bzhin 'tshal zhes gzhon nu nor bzangs kyis rgyal po las nyan nas/btsun mo rnams kyi nang du   
4b3(1): song ste|yid 'phrog ma mthong ba dang /des thams cad brjed par gyur to//de nas yang rgyal pos bsgo ba dang /yang de ma 
4b4(1): thag tu thams cad brjed par gyurato//de nas mdun 'don gyis rgyal po la smras pa/lha gzhon nu nor bzangs ni yid 'phrog ma la lhag
4b5(1): par chags pas mchi rngo mi thog gi|dmag sdogs la/gzhon nu btsun mo rnams kyi nang nas byung ba dang ci nas kyang yid   
5a1(1): 'phrog ma'i drung du mi 'gro bar thal byung btang bar bgyi'o//rgyal pos blon po rnams la smras pa/
5a2(1): shes ldan dag dpung gi tshogs sta gon gyis shig/blon po rnams kyis rgyal po las mnyan nas dpung gi tshogs glang po che dang /rta dang dpung 
5a3(1): bu chung phun sum tshogs pa/tshogs chas go mtshon rnam pa du ma dang ldan pa dag sta gon byas so//de nas gzhon nu phyir byung ba dang /sgo ba gzhon 
5a4(1): nu dpung gi tshogs sta gon byas kyi song shig des smras pa/lha yid 'phrog la bltas te mchi bar bgyi'o//rgyal pos smras pa gzhon nu thogs par 
5a5(1): 'gyur gyis ma blta zhig/yab gal te de lta na ma la ltas te mchi'o//gzhon nu ma la ltos shig/des yid 'phrog ma'i gtsug gi nor bu khyer 
5a6(1): nas ma'i drung du song ste/rkang pa gnyis la gtugs smras pa/yum bdag ri 'aro ba rnams 'bebs su mchis lags kyis/gtsug gi nor bu 
5b1(1): 'di khyod kyis shin tu dben par sbos la/yid 'phrog ma gum pa la thug pa ma lags par ji lta bur gyur kyang ma stsal cig/ces de skad ce ma la bsgo nas/mchi phyag 
5b2(1): byas te dmag dpung gi tshogs dang /rol mo'i sgra'i tshogs dang /chas te rang gi yul 'das nas/ri 'or de dang ha cang yang mi ring ba zhig na shing ldan pa zhig gi drung du 
5b3(1): btang pa ste 'dug go/de'i tshe rgyal po chen po rnam thos kyi bu 'khor gnod sbyin du ma dang /'khor gnod sbyin brgya phrag du ma dang /'khor gnodasbyin stong phrag du ma dang /
5b4(1): 'khor gnod sbyin brgya stong phrag du ma dang /lam de nas gnod sbyin rnams kyi gnod sbyin kyi mdun sar chas so//de lam de nas chas pa nas/nam mkha'i lam 
5b5(1): la thogs par gyur nas/de 'di snyam du sems te/bdag gis lam 'di nas lan mang po zhig tu song na bdag gi gzhon pa lan 'ga' yang thogs par ma gyur 
5b6(1): na/gang gis na de ltar thogs par 'di la rgyu ci yod snyam pa las/des gzhon nu nor bzangs mthong nas/de 'di ji snyam du sems te/'di ni bskal pa bzang po'i 
6a1(1): @#//byang chub sems dpa' yin te/dmag la chas pas nyon mongs par 'gyur gyi 'di'i grogs byas te/ri 'or ba 'di rnams srog chags 
6a2(1): 'ga' la yang gnod par mi 'gyur bar dbab po snyam du bsams nas/gnod sbyin gyi sde dpon chen po lnga len la smras pa/lnga len tshur shog/khyod kyis gzhon nu nor bzangs 
6a3(1): ri 'or ba 'di rnams dang /yul ma bkye bar srog chags 'ga' la yang gnod pa mi bye bar phob shig/gnod sbyin gyi sde dpon  chen po lnga len gyis bka' bzhin   
6a4(1): 'tshal zhes rgyal po rnam thos (ser )kyi bu las mnyan nas lha'i dpung gi tshogs yan lag bzhi pa/shing ta la'i tshad tsam gyi skyes pa dang /ri'i tshad tsam gyi glang po che dang /
6a5(1): glang po che'i tshad tsam gyi shing rta rnams sprul to//de nas ral gri dang /tho ba dang /mda' bo che dang /mdung dang /'khor lo dang /mdung thung dang /mda' dang /dgra 
6a6(1): sta la sogs ste/mtshon cha'i khyad par rnam pa sna tshogs dang /sil dkrol gyi sgra rnam pa sna tshogs 'byin pas 'jigs pa chen po ston cing /lnga len mthu chen po'i tshogs 
6b1(1): rnams dang ri 'or phyin pa dang /glang po che dang /rta dang shing rta'i sgra dang /sil khrol gyis chogs dang /gnod sbyin rnams kyi rang gi mthus ra bdag ral bar gyur to//de nas ri 'or 
6b2(1): na gnas pa de rnams kyis dpung gi tshogs de dang ra ba dag ral ba mthong nas mchog tu ngo mtshar skyes te dris pa/dpung gi tshogs 'di gang nas 'ongs/de rnams kyis smras pa/
6b3(1): myur du sgo phye shig/gzhon nu nor bzangs 'di'i mchug gnas 'ongs te/dpung gi tshogs 'di ni de'i yin no//gal te myur du ma phye na thams cad med par 'gyur ro//de rnams kyis 
6b4(1): bdag cag rgya rnams rgyal la mi ldog ste/gzhon nu blo dang ldan la min/rgyal po gzhon nu'i tshogs rnams kyis/bdag cag 'jigs shing skrags gyur mod/
6b5(1): ces smras nas/de rnams kyi sgo phye ste/de rnams rgyal mtshan dang /ba dan bsgreng nas bum pa gang ba dang /rol mo'i sgra sna tshogs kyis gzhon nu nor bzangs bsu'o//
6b6(1): des kyang dbugs phyung nas/de rnams 'drang ba las sna tshogs sgos te/dpya thang bcad gte'u yang bzung de nas ri 'or bde rnams pha ba nas/gzhon nu nor bzangs slar 
6b7(1): log go//rgyal po nor can gyis de nyid kyi nub mo'i rmi lam na/bya rgod cig 'ongs te rgyal po'i lto dral nas rgyu ma bzangs te/grong khyer de thams cad bskor ba dang /rin po che 
7a1(1): @#/ O /sna bdun gyi khyim du 'ongs par mthong ngo //mthong nas de nas rgyal po 'jigs skrag mi dga' zhing spu zing zhes byed nas myur ba myur bar langs te mal
7a2(1): chen po la 'dug nas lag pa 'gram pa la gtad de bzhin 'di las bdag cag rgyal srid las nyams pa 'am/srog gi bar chad du ma'igyur grang snyam nas sems khongs su chud 
7a3(1): cing 'dug pa las/de rnams la ngas rmi lam bram ze 'dun na 'don la smras pa dang /des bsams pa la rmi lam de lta bu mthong na ni gdon mi za bar gzhon nus ri bor ba rnams
7a4(1): pham par byas kyi log (ba)zhig tu bstan par bya'o snyam nas smras pa/lha khyod kyis rmi lam du gzigs pa ni/bzang po ma yin lags te/gdon mi za bar 'di las lha'i
7a5(1): rgyal srid nyams pa 'am/sku 'a tshe'i bar chad du 'gyur lags so//'on kyang 'di bzlog pa'i thabs mchis te/de yang bram ze rnams kyi gsang tshig las mthong ngo //bzlog pa'i
7a6(1): thabs de ci yin/lha skyed mes tshal du brdzing dbyigs legs pa tshad la bab pa bgyis la gnas rdo thal gyis byugs ste/legs par bster nas/ri dgas phra mo mang gi khrag gis
7b1(1): bkang la|de nas lha khrus la gshegs pa na/rdzing de la them skras gcig las 'jug par bgyi'o//gcig las zhugs nas gnyis pa la 'dzeg par bgyi'o//gnyis pa las zhugs nas 
7b2(1): gsum pa la 'dzeg par bgyi'o //gsum pa la zhugs pa nas bzhi pa la 'dzeg pa bgyi'o//de nas bram ze rigs byed dang /rigs byed kyi yan lag gi pha rol tu phyin pa bzhis lha'i zhabs
7b3(1): lces bldags la mi ma yin pa'i tshil gi bdug pa bdug go|de ltar na lha'i sngon sdig byang zhing yun ring por rgyal srid skyong bar 'gyur ro//rgyal pos smras pa/de dag thams cad ni
7b4(1): nus na 'di ltar mi ma yin pa'i tshil 'di ni shin du dkon no//'dun na 'don gyis smras pa/lhad mod ba gang lags pa de dkon lags sam/rgyal pos smras pa/de ci lta bu zhig
7b5(1): yin|mdun na 'don gyis smras pa /lha 'phrog ma 'di mi 'am ci ma lags sam/rgyal pos smas pa/'dun na 'don 'di la gzhon nu'i srog 'dug gis de ltar ma smra shig/
7b6(1): des smras pa/lha khyim gyi phyir ni 'ga' btang ste/grong rdal phyir ni khyim btang ngo //yul ljongs phyir ni grong rdal te/bdag gi phyir ni sa btang ngo //zhes bgyi ba ma gsan tam/
7b7(1): rgyal po bdag nyid brtan gyur nas//gzhon nu blo ldan de la ni//gzhan zhig stsal bar byed grang gis//yid 'phrog 'di ni gum par mdzod//bdag nyid la mngon par chags pa ni
8a1(1): @#//mi byed pa/ci yang med pas des khas blangs so//de nas 'dun na 'don gyis ji skad bstan pa bzhin du bya bar brtsams ste/rdzing brkos 
8a2(1): nas rdo thal gyis byugs shing legs par bstar nas/ri dgas phra mo rnams kyi khrag sogs pa la zhugs so//sbyor ba de nor bzangs kyi btsun mo'i skyes bos shes pas/
8a3(1): de rnams dga' ba dang /yid bde ba skyes te bdag lang tsho la bab cing gzugs phun sum tshogs pa yin pas ni bdag cag dang /gzhon nu nor bzangs yongs su dga' mgur spyod par 
8a4(1): 'gyur ro//zhes de rnams rab tu dga' bar byed pa de dag yid 'phrog mas mthong nas dris pa/khyod cag ci'i phyir lhag par dga'//ci tsam na gzhan zhig gis yid 'phrog ma 
8a5(1): la ji ltar gyur pa rgyas par smra ba dang /yid 'phrog ma sdug bsngal ba dang /yid mi bde ba skyes pa nas/gzhon nu nor bzangs kyi ma ga na ba der song ste/rkang pa gnyis la btugs 
8a6(1): nas snying rje skad du sgra chu ngus don de dal bus smras pa dang /des smras pa/gal te de lta na legs par rtogs shig/bdag gis kyang brtag go/yid 'phrog mas brtags nas yang smras pa/
8b1(1): des brtags na yang bden par mthong nas/de nas des gtsug gi nor bu de dang gos shig yid 'phrog ma la byin nas smras pa/bu mo khyod dus la ma bab par ma 'gro zhig/de lta na kho mo la kham
8b2(1): chu 'byung bar mi 'gyur ro//de nas rgyal pos ji skad bstan pa'i rim pa bzhin du khrus la chas ste/khrag gis gang ba'i rdzing bur zhugs nas byung ba dang /de nas de rkang pa gnyis bram ze rnaMs
8b3(1): kyis lces bldags so//ji tsam na mi'am ci'i bu mo khrid de shog ces bsgo ba dang /de ma thag kho nar yid 'phrog ma nam mkha' la 'phags te tshigs su bcad de smras pa//bdag
8b4(1): ni reg dang phrad pa dang //bdag ni bdag dang brtses pa las//ba lang mo btags thar lta bu//da ni bdag mo 'gro bar bya//rgyal pos kyang nam mkha'i lam las 'gro ba mthong nas||
8b5(1): de skrag ste la mdun na 'don la smras pa gang gi phyir 'bad par byed pa de ma grub par mi'am ci yid 'phrog ma bros so//'dun na 'don gyis smras pa lha da ni don grub ste/sngon sdig dang bral lagaso||
8b6(1): de nas yang yid 'phrog ma nam ma kha'i lam las 'gro ba na/'di snyam du sems te/gang yang bdag gnas skabs 'di thos pa de ni drang srong des bstan pa las gyur gyis/gal te des
8b7(1): ma bstan na ni bdag bzung zhing 'gro bar mi 'gyur ba zhig/de lta bas na res shig drang srong de'i drung du 'gro snyam nas/de de'i bsti gnas su song ste rkang pa gnyis la phyag byas nas drang srong
9a1(1): @#/ O /la smras pa|drang srong chen po khyod kyis bstan pas bdag bzung ste mchis na/ma'i rig pa rnams thob cing srog gi bar du tshad du chung zhig gis ma gyur to//de bas 
9a2(1): gsol ba ni gal te rgya zhig la nor zangs bdag tshol zhing mchis na/de la bdag gi sor gdub stsol la//'di skad ces kyang gsung ba'i rigs so//gzhon nu lam yang bgrod dka' ste/nyon mongs 
9a3(1): par 'gyur gyi slar bzhud cig/gal te bzlog kyang mchis su ma btub na/de la lam bstan pa'i rigs kyis gzhon nu nor bzangs yid 'phrog mas bstan pa byang phyogs na ri nag po gsum mchis 
9a4(1): so||de dag rgal ba'i pha rol tu na yang gsum mo//de dag rgal ba'i pha rol na yang mchis so//de dag rgal ba dang ri'i rgyal po gangs can mchis so//de'i byang phyogs na ri'i rgyal po phur ba'i rtse 
9a5(1): mchis so||de nas lam sgra can dang sing ldeng can dang /rgyud gcig pa dang /rdo rje can dang /'dod pa'i dgur gyur pa'i gzugs can dang /phur pa'i dbyibs dang /sa srung bu'i dbyibs dang /
9a6(1): mig yo ba dang /rab grol te ri bo de dag khyod kyis rgol cig/ri bo seng ge can dang /rgyud gcig ba dang /phur pa'i dbyibs la ni phug nas 'jug/rdo rje can la bya ba'i rgyal pos 'jug
9a7(1): ste|thabs 'di dag gis ri bo de rnams rgal bar bgyi'o//'khrul 'khor ra gdong can dang /lug lta bu dang /skyes bu srin po ser skya'i gzugs can dag kyang gzhom par bgyi'o//phug 
9b1(1): na sbrul kha chu'i rgyun chen po'i shugs kyis rgyug pa zhig mchis te/de ni zho shas gzhom par bgyi'o//gang na sbrul ni nag po zhig/phug na phyed 'dug mthong nas ni/bdag gi slad du 
9b2(1): gzhu gdungs la//mnga' yis bsnun te bsad par bgyis//gang na lug gnyis phan tshun nas//brdungs bar byed pa mthong na ni//de gnyis rang re bcag brjod dang //lam ni rnyed 
9b3(1): par 'gyur lags so//'jigs tshe skyed mchog thogs pa yi//lcags kyi skyes pa gnyis mthong nas//de gnyis las ni gcig brdegs dang //lam ni rnyed par 'gyur lags so//
9b4(1): srin po lcags kyi chu can zhig/kha 'dzums gdang ba bgyid gzugs na//de tshe de'i dpral ba la//phur pa dag gis gdab par mdzod//de bzhin khron pa klong dag khyil//drug cu'i 
9b5(1): khru can la khyod mchongs//skra mig seng ge ltar ser zhing //mi zad bsnyen par dka' ba'i//gnod sbyin dang ni srin po dag/gzhu ni bkang ste bsad mdzod la//chu srin 'dzin khris 
9b6(1): gang ba yi//chu bo mang po rgal mdzod cig/nang na phem leb gdungs dang //sna tshogs dud mo dgong blang dang //sbrul can 'od ma can gyi klung //nang gan na ni srin mo khro//phye ma leb dang mi ma 
9b7(1): yin||gdungs pa na ni 'dzin khri mang //sna tshogs can 'dod sgyur gzugs//dud mor ma'am ci'i bran//dgod pa na ni mi'am ci//gna' ma sbrum ma sbrul can na//'od ma can klung 
10a1(1): @#/ O /shal ma li//nad ga la ni brtan byar bya//phye ma le ba la rtul phod bya//gdung bar 'dzin khri kha bcing ngo //sna tshogs can du klu sna tshogs//dud mo can du yin
10a2(1): bde bas//dgod byed du ni mi smra ba'i//sbyor bas kun tu brgal bar mdzod//sbrul can du ni sbrul bdug gi/sngags kyi sbyor dang 'od ma can//klung du mtshon rnon gda' ba yi//
10a3(1): sbyor bas kun tu rgal ba'i mdzod//chu rnams rgal nas bcag gnas na//gnod sbyin lnga brgya yod pa de//rnams la sran tshugs gzhom mdzod cig/de nas ni mi'am ci'i 
10a4(1): rgyal po'i pho brang mchis so//zhes gsung zhig/drang srong de la yid 'phrog mas de skad ces smras nas//rkang pa gnyis la mgo bos btugs te song ngo //ci tsam na gzhon nu nor 
10a5(1): bzangs kyi ri 'or Ide phab nas skyes khyer te glang po'i grong khyer du 'ongs so//zhes rgyal pos thos nas/mchog tu dga' ba skyes so//de nas gzhon nu ngal sos nas pha'i drung du song 
10a6(1): ste༐ phyag byas mdun du 'dug pa dang /rgyal pos mchog tu dga' bar 'gyur pa'i gtam byas nas smras pa/gzhon nu khyod bde bar 'ongs sam/lha khyod kyis bka' drin gyis 
10a7(1): ri 'phab ste ste'u bzung nas sna bo skos lags te dpya dang skyes kyang 'di dag lags kyis phyag mdzod du gzhug tu gsol/rgyal pos smras pa/bu legs par byas kyis bzhe(sa)
10b1(1): so||de nas gzhon nus phal phyag byas nas chas pa dang /rgyal pos smras pa/gzhon nu skyes la lhan cig kho nar longs spyod kyis 'dug shig/lha bdag gis yid 'phrog ma 
10b2(1): mthong nas yun ring du zhig legs pas mchi'o lags so//gzhon nu deng 'gro bshol la sang song shig/de gal chung zhing 'di skad ces smras so//yab nges par deng nyid bdag mchi 
10b3(1): lags so//rgyal po cang mi smra bar 'dug go/de nas gzhon nu rang gi khyim du song bas/btsun mo pho brang gi sgo dpal kyang stong par mthong ba nas/de sems khongs su chud bzhin du 
10b4(1): zhugs na/yid 'phrog ma ma mthong nas snying stor te/phan tshun du rgyu zhing yid 'phrog ma zhes sgra byung ngo //ci tsam na btsun mo rnams 'tshogs nas/btsun mo de rnams kyi gta 
10b5(1): glur bskur ba dang /de'i snying la gzug rdus gzug pas yang nas yang du dris pa las/de rnams kyis ji ltar gyur pa smras so//de mya ngan las yid klong bar gyur te/bud med de rnams kyis 
10b6(1): smra pa|lha btsun mo'i 'khor 'di ni/de bas bud med shin tu khyad par 'phags pa dag mchis na/ci'i slad thugs ngan mdzad/des snying rje med cing byas pa chud son pa thob nas ma'i 
10b7(1): drung du song ste/rkang pa la gtugs nas smras pa/yum yid bzhin yon tan ldan gyur cig/gzugs mdzes phun sum tshogs pa yi//yid 'phrog ma ni mi 'da' na//bdag gi yid 
11a1(1): 'phrog gang du mchis//yid kyis rab tu brgyugs snyam na'ang //bdag gi yid ni kun tu rmongs//de dang bral bar gyur pa yi//snying ni shin tu gdungs pa nyid//
11a2(1): bdag gi yid du che zhing yid 'phrog la//yid du 'thad cing yid la dga' ba yi//bdag nyid sdug bsngal 'di ni gang gnas mchis//des smras pa bu yid 'phrog ma sdug bsngal mi zad pa 
11a3(1): nyam nga bar gyur nas kho mos btang ngo //yum ji ltar gyur pa/des ji ltar gyur pa thams cad rgyas par smras pa dang /des pha snying rje med cing byas pa mi bzo ba yin no zhes smras nas smras 
11a4(1): pa|yum gang du mchis pa/shul ni gang nas des smras pa gang du yid 'phrog song ba yi/lam de ri dang drang srong dang /seng ge dag gis brtag brten cing /chos kyi rgyal po gnas pa yin|
11a5(1): de yid 'phrog ma dang bral ba'i sdug bsngal gyis mi bzod par nyams thag cing snying rje'i skad du bla zhing smre sngags 'don pa/yid bzhin yon tan ldan pa gyur pa'i//yid 'phrog ma ni mi 
11a6(1): 'dar na||zhes bya ba nas bdag gi sdug bsngal 'di ni gang nas 'ongs zhes bya ba'i bar gong ma bzhin no//de nas mas smras pa/bu btsun mo'i 'khor 'di ni de bas ches khyad par du 
11a7(1): 'phags pa'i bud med dag yod na/ci'i phyir mya ngan byed/gzhon nus smras pa/yum de ma rnyed par bdag dga' bar ga la 'gyur/des de dbugs phyung yang 'on kyang mya ngan gyis 
11b1(1): gdungs nas de'i khungs tshol zhing phan tshun du btsal bar brtsams te/de'i blo skyes pa/gang kho nas thob pa de nyid la re shig dri'o snyam nas des sngon pa spang leb can gyi drung du song ste dris 
11b2(1): pa khyod kyis yid 'phrog ma gang nas rnyed des smras pa/ri bo che ge mo'i ngos la drang srong zhig gnas te/de'i bsti gnas na tshangs pa 'du ba zhes bgyi ba'i rdzing mchis te/der khrus la 
11b3(1): zhugs pa las/drang srong gi bstan nas rnyed de//des bsams pa/da ni drang srong sru+ong dang /de las khungs phyin par 'gyur ro snyam mo//rgyal pos gzhon nu ni yid 'phrog ma dang bral 
11b4(1): bas shin tu yid blong ba'i gtam thos so//de nas rgyal pos smras pa/gzhon nu khyod yid blong du ci yod/da ni de bas khyad par du 'phags pa'i chung ma byin no//des smras pa/yab 
11b5(1): bdag ga ni de ma lags pa'i mchis brang gi 'khor dang mchis par rngo mi thogs lags so//rgyal pos de la lam du ma smras kyang ma zlogs so//de nas rgyal pos gring khyer gyi sgo
